# Excalidraw Data
## Text Elements
Regardless of what units are used to quantify mass, every one of them provides some measure of mass.
We can therefore label the general DIMENSION of mass [M] ^o6QulBVi

"Dimensional homogeneity" is a fancy way of saying that LHS=RHS.
We can use it to find the units of a constant. ^vILlnw1T

Dimensional analysis can help us remember expressions or deduce relationships. ^TjND7SzZ

Angles are ALWAYS dimensionless
Exponentials are as well ^b2DrEysH

Addition and subtraction. All the terms should have the same dimension. ^br9dGrSP

[[Year 1]] ^2BIzXGFQ
